Triggers: "premortem", "事前チェック", "実行前チェック", "リスク判定", "pre-flight check", "run premortem". - **premortem**: Runs a premortem on a plan before committing to it — imagines the plan has already failed spectacularly and works backward to the causes. Use when the user says /premortem, "run a premortem," "assume this failed, why," "what could kill this," "imagine this went wrong," or before committing to anything expensive or hard to reverse. Distinct from redteam: redteam argues against the plan now; premortem time-travels to the failure and traces the path there, which surfaces different, often more concrete risks. Produces the top failure paths ranked by likelihood-times-damage, the earliest warning s… ([jbookout/carr-system](https://github.com/jbookout/carr-system/tree/HEAD/corpus/_home/.claude/skills/premortem))
- **premortem**: Premortem before substantial work.
